# Brands are acting strange this Christmas. Something wicked is going on… and it’s bigger than the Las Vegas Sphere!

## Metadata
- Author: [[Jaskaran from The Social Juice]]
- Full Title: Brands are acting strange this Christmas. Something wicked is going on… and it’s bigger than the Las Vegas Sphere!
- Category: #articles
- Summary: Brands are staging bold, weird, and highly creative holiday campaigns across the globe. Big moves, mergers, and AI-driven retail shifts are reshaping marketing and budgets. The season shows both nostalgic cheer and growing pressure for measurable ROI.

##### It’s the best and worst season of marketing. The campaigns are so chilling I need a new scarf, and my car’s in Vegas, so I can’t even shop for Ralph Lauren Christmas. What’s going on?

This newsletter is supported by our friends at [Metricool](https://substack.com/redirect/6c169780-7da7-442b-bebf-e8eca4d4da73?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g). They’re organising a social media summit that’s free, virtual, and unfiltered, just like this newsletter.

> Brand & Advertising coverage from 09.11 — 21.11.2025, with a new section covering the ongoing Christmas season.

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!JnHP!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F01e1bfca-7b3a-4e58-80e6-f8bb4cb10eb2_1100x220.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/7cdddaef-55ea-4a12-8d1c-adc3d1923c99?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* Adobe [to acquire](https://substack.com/redirect/fc8ce2fd-5005-483c-9b2f-4f47a81fa86e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) digital marketing platform Semrush for $1.9 billion.
* [Ulta Beauty’s Strip Mall dominance](https://substack.com/redirect/2afa4f8c-8946-47dc-af6b-60e55aea7603?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) made it hub for makeup and beauty.
* China’s Singles’ Day shopping festival winds down with ‘[muted](https://substack.com/redirect/639f173d-6a15-413d-9efb-ed6c1be06c3a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ sentiment.
* Would you buy books from [Shein](https://substack.com/redirect/6ef13bc3-6c33-4a1b-9449-af84157f848c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)?
* Ozempic users are [going crazy](https://substack.com/redirect/2a3c63d3-a72e-46a1-af47-f46a175c4a6d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for deli meat, fries, and handbags.
* Welcome to [Our New Era](https://substack.com/redirect/67cd4c98-560a-4197-997a-f10f5d47ee39?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g). What Do We Call It?
* Gen Z is huge. Their [TV Shows are tiny](https://substack.com/redirect/f2181028-6847-4a96-8d1e-f93007e54504?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g). And Hollywood is panicking.
* [Bill Ackman](https://substack.com/redirect/5f18cbf3-f50f-481d-9b5b-14f57c9600bf?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) shared dating advice for young men. Not everyone swooned.
* Young Mormons built an app to help men [quit Gooning](https://substack.com/redirect/3e36352f-2e9c-44fe-9e4c-eb9934e54ffa?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* [Quarter Zip](https://substack.com/redirect/36d5a381-8ed9-40e9-be80-a7046d9b3c7f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) viral fashion trend, explained.
* The Rise of [Luxury Fashion](https://substack.com/redirect/daad6805-ee8f-4113-949d-a6fba3774b5c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) Restaurants.

*In partnership with [Metricool](https://substack.com/redirect/6c169780-7da7-442b-bebf-e8eca4d4da73?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)*

##### The best things in life are free. And also, this event about social media is free and [unfiltered](https://substack.com/redirect/6c169780-7da7-442b-bebf-e8eca4d4da73?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!mIMF!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F5f2318d0-00ef-4f8a-a450-b546ca4ca348_1894x991.jpeg)](https://substack.com/redirect/7fe42969-bfb5-402c-862b-3be5333b4913?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)I’ll be honest with you. When [Metricool](https://substack.com/redirect/6c169780-7da7-442b-bebf-e8eca4d4da73?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) first reached out to me about this free event, I was a bit hesitant. *I’ve used their social media tool for years and it’s amazing.* But you know, they always did the work quietly, and you didn’t hear much about the [cool events](https://substack.com/redirect/6c169780-7da7-442b-bebf-e8eca4d4da73?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g). But man, was I wrong. [The speakers](https://substack.com/redirect/6c169780-7da7-442b-bebf-e8eca4d4da73?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) at their events are some of the coolest and most insightful people I know.

Our frequent collaborator on Instagram, **Dakota Rae Lowe**, VP of Social Strategy at Edelman, is giving a talk about **Decoding Internet Lore to Create Brand Meaning**.

**Alex Frolov**, CEO of HypeAuditor, is giving a talk about data-driven creator marketing. If you work in influencer marketing and know some gossip, you know *HypeAuditor is basically the Cloudflare* *of this industry*. Pretty much everything runs through it.

**Sara Di Costanzo**, Sr Strategic Partnerships Manager, Platform and Agency at Pinterest, will talk about the **Pinterest Predicts Report 2026**. I don’t need to convince you on this one. I know my analytics, and everyone loves that report. A talk about it is already a win.

These three guests are absolute bangers, but the rest of the speakers list is also stacked: Matt Navarra, Olivia Mae Hanlon, Mark Erickson, Brock Johnson, Arielle Berlinsky, Tomas Loucky, Latasha James, and Xandrina Allday.

Now, they didn’t even need to pay me to tell you this because I always share [Metricool’s social media studies](https://substack.com/redirect/6c169780-7da7-442b-bebf-e8eca4d4da73?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) whenever they come out. You can catch a first look at the new one by joining this free event. [Every session](https://substack.com/redirect/6c169780-7da7-442b-bebf-e8eca4d4da73?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) will have a live Q&A at the end so there will be multiple opportunities for attendees to ask questions.

> Date: December 10, 2025 • Time: 9 AM EST • Format: Free, virtual

> [Register](https://substack.com/redirect/6c169780-7da7-442b-bebf-e8eca4d4da73?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for the [Unfiltered event](https://substack.com/redirect/6c169780-7da7-442b-bebf-e8eca4d4da73?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) at no cost and join us live.

* U.S. envoy warns Canada to ‘[seriously consider](https://substack.com/redirect/582c61e0-a84d-4300-be2f-8af9611ae6bb?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ avoiding anti-tariff ads.
* Toy makers rush into ‘[blind box](https://substack.com/redirect/7b861928-0bb2-44a6-85ea-feb69f190c42?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ trend for holidays following Labubu craze.
* [Verizon](https://substack.com/redirect/7d155e59-59ff-4ced-8411-b29885ac4125?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to cut about 15,000 jobs.
* [Marketers are quitting](https://substack.com/redirect/b0740f3a-3c1c-44a6-9b16-dd9f8c05334c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) corporations for sports teams. CMOs who made the move explain why.
* At Loyola Marymount’s KXLU, [College Radio](https://substack.com/redirect/7f0f212c-a2c3-494d-88a1-5411b127cbdd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) keeps its cool.
* Like [Goop](https://substack.com/redirect/3c465ff6-c702-4852-b39b-1886f7255611?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), but for Dogs.
* Havas responds to deal reports, [denying](https://substack.com/redirect/a1abbe41-9f6b-429d-9e5e-f056a4ec3046?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) talks with WPP.
* Forget sports betting. [Kalshi](https://substack.com/redirect/0314d9b0-939b-4ef1-8918-36d493e88d56?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) wants you to wager on sneakers and Labubu dolls..
* [Where](https://substack.com/redirect/fdc56832-830b-4c4a-b205-f7123f1bc56d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) Do the Children Play?
* The [Silicon Valley Beauty Brand](https://substack.com/redirect/25d4008d-cf2b-4ea8-ac3d-d8419278840e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) biohackers are obsessed with.
* CMOs see [support for brand building fade](https://substack.com/redirect/a8a5522a-a5d4-4c58-aa17-19f836a68c8e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) while ROI scrutiny rises: [NIQ](https://substack.com/redirect/b99b2d6f-3b94-4ae6-9afd-fff6c265e8d4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
	+ Only [69% of CMOs](https://substack.com/redirect/b99b2d6f-3b94-4ae6-9afd-fff6c265e8d4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) say their CEO and chief financial officer (CFO) support long-term brand investment, a sharp decline from 80% last year.
* Why [socks in Japan](https://substack.com/redirect/65ae3049-ae99-4b00-ac97-3423a77c038a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) are a serious business.
* Marketers move to bring [transparency](https://substack.com/redirect/5a58c08f-305d-4e74-864e-667d13b5a7da?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to creator and influencer fees.
* Is fashion ready for the [AI Bubble](https://substack.com/redirect/4c372018-0749-49b0-8575-01bea61f3d3a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to burst?

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!_L36!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F22cabaf0-a437-483c-a9fb-acf71ce426f2_1100x220.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/18a9f584-b91e-4a8d-b029-8c0642fb143c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* What’s happening at The Social Juice?
	+ It’s the Las Vegas Grand Prix. I tried to curate some of the best brand activations so far at the [Sphere Vegas](https://substack.com/redirect/9f38d185-6db1-4d4f-b707-dd301f49100b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g). My biased take is that Google and LEGO keep dominating with their incredible activations.
	+ [Wicked](https://substack.com/redirect/aadc0354-ab9a-4f39-85f2-9ca8b1ab5974?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) has partnered with over 400 brands and counting. They even opened [an experience center?](https://substack.com/redirect/6e1a3dda-fa4a-4e2b-b75b-96a40eb1e488?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to showcase their collaborations, and it’s just crazy. 
		- Over 300 people reacted to this post, and people are divided. Some say it’s the culture; some say capitalism. Our friend Ashley did great videos on both topics: [Wicked](https://substack.com/redirect/09ef9f4c-26ae-40f9-8050-4b230880804a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) and [brand love vs. capitalism](https://substack.com/redirect/a2a3b685-5476-4615-87e2-d7cce69219cc?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
	+ [Zootopia 2](https://substack.com/redirect/29dcd1fe-c976-4183-b674-3e36cebf29cc?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) also had good but minimal movie marketing moments. If you haven’t seen them, you should.
	+ [Christmas campaigns](https://substack.com/redirect/5d7317e8-6e54-4b6f-ad62-6692fc59df6e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) have divided people this year, but the social media engagement winner seems to be [Waitrose](https://substack.com/redirect/769cceee-7429-4307-8087-11db9a26ddc6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
	+ We didn’t get time to make the Stranger Things collaboration post yet, but the [collaborations](https://substack.com/redirect/24de952e-ddbb-4c73-b411-a12eb330df79?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) listed below have some highs and lows. The [KFC activation](https://substack.com/redirect/91c509a1-aecd-47c2-a0ae-37fc7c5527a5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) is my personal favourite.
	+ [Sony Playstation](https://substack.com/redirect/f15a09a4-961a-49b6-97b0-c6584a761e27?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) is celebrating different milestones in different regions with campaigns that remind me of 2000s marketing. Meanwhile, Xbox’s marketing strategy of “[This is an Xbox](https://substack.com/redirect/6e56e3ba-8d2d-46ad-8d7f-173ead8777b4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)” is a mixed bag even for their biggest fans.
	+ Coincidence or planned? The Ralph Lauren pop-up at Chelsea couldn’t be [more timely](https://substack.com/redirect/dc25a9ea-9e04-4b51-9e47-bc22d9c0bc73?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
	+ And someone is planning a comeback?
		- [BeReal](https://substack.com/redirect/cbab4701-b053-43c1-858a-a0967f864f81?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) OOH campaigns [kick off](https://substack.com/redirect/93a7002d-2c75-4b19-a1bd-d8bc825beff9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in Paris, New York.
* [Don’t get them a Yeti](https://substack.com/redirect/d929096f-a705-41c9-a00d-6a3aad369b5c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) says Yeti ad campaign.
* No, you’re not seeing things: ‘[UFO landings](https://substack.com/redirect/158088a1-60ab-4413-be01-042d249ba079?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ and sightings of ‘mysterious creatures’ are happening across the world in real life as part of Sony’s latest PS5 push.
* “[A Very ALDI Holiday Pop-Up](https://substack.com/redirect/201852ee-bb64-41c0-9c82-50d0e4775c92?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)” is scheduled for Dec. 3 as the discount grocer prepares flagship Manhattan store.

---

> 😹 Report Break: Salesforce reports that the 2025 Cyber Week, Thursday, November 27 through Monday, December 1, is expected to drive a record-breaking [$334 billion in global sales](https://substack.com/redirect/f9fc3454-2e8e-43ba-b79b-eea32d3f0a19?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), $73 billion of which is attributed to the influence of AI and [agents](https://substack.com/redirect/3efdfef6-33a4-4a77-81ac-59be93f6ecf5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) (up 22% from 2024). Retailers with agents on their own properties already experienced seven times the U.S. sales growth (13%) compared with those without (2%) in the last seven weeks.

* Exclusive: [Primark](https://substack.com/redirect/fb0683ce-9530-4436-b257-546cdeb76196?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) signs PR agency to new creative brief.
* Dyson [launches](https://substack.com/redirect/93838b44-465c-498c-b754-c08069b61710?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) review of $500m media account.
* Ikea and Mother [part ways](https://substack.com/redirect/f4b2c34d-fe2b-4feb-9780-e9fee46923f6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) after 16 years.
* [Playstation](https://substack.com/redirect/1512533a-11c8-4c7d-bbe7-22b3a144699d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) breaks the fourth wall, with Akcelo, EssenceMediacom and Apparition.
* Don Smallgoods appoints [Grapevine](https://substack.com/redirect/a2c7dacf-19fa-4aa7-b27d-16430eab2747?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) as new social media agency.
* EssenceMediacom [retains](https://substack.com/redirect/9c4140a1-45cb-4051-ae12-a808201777c8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) $15m NRL account.
* Salty Games appoints [Orange Line](https://substack.com/redirect/cbf71391-a29a-4d7a-b1f5-95689a6c64d5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) as new digital media and creative agency of record.
* Take 5 Oil changes up [its agency roster](https://substack.com/redirect/1b23136c-6693-4405-9883-150cb7023f83?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) amid brand overhaul.
* Chicken Farmers of Canada names [No Fixed Address](https://substack.com/redirect/d55e1770-0dec-4085-a48e-100c2337eb14?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) Creative Agency of Record.
* [Tonies](https://substack.com/redirect/b593648d-b9c6-4c3e-b77b-c6069c0b1ab6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) taps Connected Media for media planning.
* Rush Street Gaming taps [Doner](https://substack.com/redirect/25486810-20a6-4e20-9057-476fd516161c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) as new AOR.
* Royal Ontario Museum selects [Courage](https://substack.com/redirect/61e06c48-e5d5-4c08-88ae-092dcd7b3f79?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) as creative partner.
* [Media33](https://substack.com/redirect/f7d0f5ba-ff6e-4889-a7e2-60f60e5048ec?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) wins Las Vegas Raiders media account.
* Tourism and Events Queensland appoints [Jaywing](https://substack.com/redirect/3e893dc9-a991-440c-8e66-669771e5d73c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) as SEO agency for Queensland.com.
* TBWA\NZ lands [Haier account](https://substack.com/redirect/3fa8d266-0a9c-4545-b868-02a5894d7cfc?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) across ANZ and launches first Australian Open campaign.
* [Barrett Hofherr](https://substack.com/redirect/f49aa936-4309-433f-979c-4ea757de2fec?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) selected to create Bachan’s debut brand campaign.
* [STIHL](https://substack.com/redirect/7cddc353-aa76-4ea4-be6d-a568f98301e2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) names The Martin Agency as Creative Agency of Record.
* Royal Mail [shortlists](https://substack.com/redirect/8eec70d0-9b42-4909-92f3-3a87b2d6e067?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) three creative agencies in review.
* Carman’s Kitchen appoints [Kill Boring Dead](https://substack.com/redirect/9d305ee7-dc73-481c-bfd3-4f7865556be7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) as social-first creative agency, unveils new work.
* Eurovision crowns [AKQA](https://substack.com/redirect/7100dce0-e534-48b5-99ef-4ece1b210bfb?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to ‘reimagine’ fan experience at 70 years.
* [VaynerMedia](https://substack.com/redirect/9d29e092-7f3d-4728-8585-3ba65dbc2cee?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) named lead social agency for HBO Max Australia.
* [Media Words](https://substack.com/redirect/b497da81-3caf-43c2-b0c6-290708d448fc?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) wins Fisherman’s Friend account.
* AJ Bell has appointed [VML](https://substack.com/redirect/8992b73e-14ce-473f-9d7f-1fb7cd57c9e0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to drive its CRM transformation.
* Müller Yoghurt and Desserts has appointed [Coolr](https://substack.com/redirect/80b29158-9f60-4339-b669-c54301665a75?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) as its social media agency of record.
* Social Chain’s influencer arm joins the [Loveholidays](https://substack.com/redirect/d8881604-2e50-4279-9ecb-5796cf80f05e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) growth journey.
* [Bensons for Beds](https://substack.com/redirect/abbb9d83-2604-4039-9fbb-a9725dc10b87?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) selects Wavemaker as media agency.
* Land O’Frost selects [MissionOne Media](https://substack.com/redirect/dd5eb3d2-e1e0-4745-86c0-3aef917d8592?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) as Media Agency of Record.
* [Samsung](https://substack.com/redirect/114e0f79-bbce-4df0-873a-4c2473962f40?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) set to appoint agency for UK social account.
* Stagecoach appoints [Bountiful Cow](https://substack.com/redirect/50077a6c-4c01-42ff-9a6a-ca38960a45b0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) as strategic media partner.
* Volvo sister brand Geely kicks off [UK media review](https://substack.com/redirect/62621e43-76dc-4566-85a5-c19e52e19297?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* TEG appoints [iProspect](https://substack.com/redirect/078c05bc-5174-4dd9-837e-028f4c90708c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) as media agency after eight-year run with Zenith.
* Tropical Smoothie Cafe selects [Fallon](https://substack.com/redirect/b7d5359d-57b5-46c2-af00-1fecb78aa21a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) as Creative Agency of Record

---

Back on Sunday. Until then…

When you can also expense it:* [Generate](https://substack.com/redirect/cecca8c3-2ccd-46bc-9b88-93c0fff92682?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) an Employee Expense Email for Your Manager.

<h1 dir="auto" style="direction: auto;text-align: start;unicode-bidi: isolate;color: rgb(54,55,55);font-family: 'SF Pro Display', -apple-system, system-ui, BlinkMacSystemFont, 'Inter', 'Segoe UI', Roboto, Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif, 'Apple Color Emoji', 'Segoe UI Emoji', 'Segoe UI Symbol';font-weight: 900;-webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;-moz-osx-font-smoothing: antialiased;-webkit-appearance: optimizelegibility;-moz-appearance: optimizelegibility;appearance: optimizelegibility;margin: 0;line-height: 36px;font-size: 32px;"><a href="https://substack.com/app-link/post?publication\_id=712314&amp;post\_id=179536951&amp;utm\_source=post-email-title&amp;utm\_campaign=email-post-title&amp;isFreemail=true&amp;r=6q6egl&amp;token=eyJ1c2VyX2lkIjo0MDY3NjU3NDksInBvc3RfaWQiOjE3OTUzNjk1MSwiaWF0IjoxNzYzNzY1OTgzLCJleHAiOjE3NjYzNTc5ODMsImlzcyI6InB1Yi03MTIzMTQiLCJzdWIiOiJwb3N0LXJlYWN0aW9uIn0.Hmuke8lgBk8YWcvQ6JZ9VAQ8vUQbVHofJkdE7eYNhgg" style="color: rgb(54,55,55);text-decoration: none;">Brands are acting strange this Christmas. Something wicked is going on… and it’s bigger than the Las Vegas Sphere!</a></h1>
<h3 dir="auto" style="direction: auto;text-align: start;unicode-bidi: isolate;font-family: 'SF Pro Display',-apple-system-headline,system-ui,-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',Roboto,Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif,'Apple Color Emoji','Segoe UI Emoji','Segoe UI Symbol';font-weight: normal;-webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;-moz-osx-font-smoothing: antialiased;-webkit-appearance: optimizelegibility;-moz-appearance: optimizelegibility;appearance: optimizelegibility;margin: 4px 0 0;color: #777777;line-height: 24px;font-size: 18px;margin-top: 12px;">It’s the best and worst season of marketing. The campaigns are so chilling I need a new scarf, and my car’s in Vegas, so I can’t even shop for Ralph Lauren Christmas. What’s going on?</h3>
<table border="0" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" role="presentation" style="margin: 1em 0;height: 20px;align-items: center;" width="100%"><tbody><tr>

<div dir="auto" style="--image-offset-margin: -120px;padding: 32px 0 0 0;font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><div dir="auto" style="text-align: initial;font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;width: 100%;word-break: break-word;margin-bottom: 16px;font-family: Spectral,sans-serif;font-weight: 400;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;margin-top: 0;"><span>This newsletter is supported by our friends at </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/6c169780-7da7-442b-bebf-e8eca4d4da73?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Metricool</a><span>. They’re organising a social media summit that’s free, virtual, and unfiltered, just like this newsletter.</span></p>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;"><p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">Brand &amp; Advertising coverage from 09.11 — 21.11.2025, with a new section covering the ongoing Christmas season.</p></blockquote>
<p data-attrs='{"url":"https://thesocialjuice.substack.com/bec55781","text":"The Social Juice+ (more of what matters)","action":null,"class":"button-wrapper"}' data-component-name="ButtonCreateButton" style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;text-align: center;cursor: pointer;border-radius: 4px;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/42402d43-446f-4e24-8b4c-16d52c145791?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="font-family: system-ui,-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',Roboto,Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif,'Apple Color Emoji','Segoe UI Emoji','Segoe UI Symbol';display: inline-block;box-sizing: border-box;border: none;font-size: 14px;line-height: 20px;font-weight: 600;margin: 0;opacity: 1;outline: none;white-space: nowrap;color: #ffffff !important;text-decoration: none !important;text-align: center;cursor: pointer;border-radius: 4px;background-color: #e85c57;padding: 12px 20px;height: auto;"><span style="color: #ffffff;text-decoration: none;">The Social Juice+ (more of what matters)</span></a></p>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;margin: 32px auto;"><figure style="width: 100%;margin: 0 auto;"><table border="0" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" data-component-name="Image2ToDOMStatic" style="mso-padding-alt: 1em 0 1.6em;" width="100%"><tbody><tr>

<td align="left" style="text-align: center;" width="1100"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/7cdddaef-55ea-4a12-8d1c-adc3d1923c99?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="position: relative;flex-direction: column;align-items: center;padding: 0;width: auto;height: auto;border: none;text-decoration: none;display: block;margin: 0;" target="\_blank"><img alt="" data-attrs='{"src":"https://substack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/01e1bfca-7b3a-4e58-80e6-f8bb4cb10eb2\_1100x220.png","srcNoWatermark":null,"fullscreen":null,"imageSize":null,"height":220,"width":1100,"resizeWidth":null,"bytes":67342,"alt":null,"title":null,"type":"image/png","href":null,"belowTheFold":false,"topImage":true,"internalRedirect":"https://thesocialjuice.substack.com/i/164226189?img=https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F01e1bfca-7b3a-4e58-80e6-f8bb4cb10eb2\_1100x220.png","isProcessing":false,"align":null,"offset":false}' height="110" src="https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/%24s\_!JnHP!,w\_1100,c\_limit,f\_auto,q\_auto:good,fl\_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F01e1bfca-7b3a-4e58-80e6-f8bb4cb10eb2\_1100x220.png" style="border: none !important;vertical-align: middle;display: block;-ms-interpolation-mode: bicubic;height: auto;margin-bottom: 0;width: auto !important;max-width: 100% !important;margin: 0 auto;" width="550"/></a></td>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
</tr></tbody></table></figure></div>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Adobe </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/fc8ce2fd-5005-483c-9b2f-4f47a81fa86e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">to acquire</a><span> digital marketing platform Semrush for $1.9 billion.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/2afa4f8c-8946-47dc-af6b-60e55aea7603?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Ulta Beauty’s Strip Mall dominance</a><span> made it hub for makeup and beauty.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>China’s Singles’ Day shopping festival winds down with ‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/639f173d-6a15-413d-9efb-ed6c1be06c3a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">muted</a><span>’ sentiment.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Would you buy books from </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/6ef13bc3-6c33-4a1b-9449-af84157f848c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Shein</a><span>?</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Ozempic users are </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/2a3c63d3-a72e-46a1-af47-f46a175c4a6d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">going crazy</a><span> for deli meat, fries, and handbags.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Welcome to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/67cd4c98-560a-4197-997a-f10f5d47ee39?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Our New Era</a><span>. What Do We Call It?</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Gen Z is huge. Their </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f2181028-6847-4a96-8d1e-f93007e54504?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">TV Shows are tiny</a><span>. And Hollywood is panicking.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/5f18cbf3-f50f-481d-9b5b-14f57c9600bf?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Bill Ackman</a><span> shared dating advice for young men. Not everyone swooned.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Young Mormons built an app to help men </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/3e36352f-2e9c-44fe-9e4c-eb9934e54ffa?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">quit Gooning</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/36d5a381-8ed9-40e9-be80-a7046d9b3c7f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Quarter Zip</a><span> viral fashion trend, explained.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>The Rise of </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/daad6805-ee8f-4113-949d-a6fba3774b5c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Luxury Fashion</a><span> Restaurants.</span></p></li>
</ul>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><hr style="margin: 32px 0;padding: 0;height: 1px;background: #e6e6e6;border: none;"/></div>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><em><span>In partnership with </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/6c169780-7da7-442b-bebf-e8eca4d4da73?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Metricool</a></em></p>
<h3 style="position: relative;font-family: 'SF Pro Display',-apple-system-headline,system-ui,-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',Roboto,Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif,'Apple Color Emoji','Segoe UI Emoji','Segoe UI Symbol';font-weight: bold;-webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;-moz-osx-font-smoothing: antialiased;-webkit-appearance: optimizelegibility;-moz-appearance: optimizelegibility;appearance: optimizelegibility;margin: 1em 0 0.625em 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 1.16em;font-size: 1.375em;">
<span>The best things in life are free. And also, this event about social media is free and </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/6c169780-7da7-442b-bebf-e8eca4d4da73?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: rgb(54,55,55);text-decoration: underline;">unfiltered</a><span>.</span>
</h3>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;margin: 32px auto;"><figure style="width: 100%;margin: 0 auto;"><table border="0" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" data-component-name="Image2ToDOMStatic" style="mso-padding-alt: 1em 0 1.6em;" width="100%"><tbody><tr>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
<td align="left" style="text-align: center;" width="558"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/7fe42969-bfb5-402c-862b-3be5333b4913?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="position: relative;flex-direction: column;align-items: center;padding: 0;width: auto;height: auto;border: none;text-decoration: none;display: block;margin: 0;" target="\_blank"><img alt="" data-attrs='{"src":"https://substack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/5f2318d0-00ef-4f8a-a450-b546ca4ca348\_1894x991.jpeg","srcNoWatermark":null,"fullscreen":false,"imageSize":"normal","height":991,"width":1894,"resizeWidth":558,"bytes":295239,"alt":null,"title":null,"type":"image/jpeg","href":null,"belowTheFold":false,"topImage":false,"internalRedirect":"https://thesocialjuice.substack.com/i/179536951?img=https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fc6dae798-9715-4412-9ea0-24176d76584d\_1920x1080.jpeg","isProcessing":false,"align":"center","offset":false}' height="287.7771911298838" src="https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/%24s\_!mIMF!,w\_1100,c\_limit,f\_auto,q\_auto:good,fl\_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F5f2318d0-00ef-4f8a-a450-b546ca4ca348\_1894x991.jpeg" style="border: none !important;vertical-align: middle;display: block;-ms-interpolation-mode: bicubic;height: auto;margin-bottom: 0;width: auto !important;max-width: 100% !important;margin: 0 auto;" width="550"/></a></td>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
</tr></tbody></table></figure></div>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>I’ll be honest with you. When </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/6c169780-7da7-442b-bebf-e8eca4d4da73?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Metricool</a><span> first reached out to me about this free event, I was a bit hesitant. </span><em>I’ve used their social media tool for years and it’s amazing.</em><span> But you know, they always did the work quietly, and you didn’t hear much about the </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/6c169780-7da7-442b-bebf-e8eca4d4da73?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">cool events</a><span>. But man, was I wrong. </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/6c169780-7da7-442b-bebf-e8eca4d4da73?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">The speakers</a><span> at their events are some of the coolest and most insightful people I know.</span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Our frequent collaborator on Instagram, </span><strong>Dakota Rae Lowe</strong><span>, VP of Social Strategy at Edelman, is giving a talk about </span><strong>Decoding Internet Lore to Create Brand Meaning</strong><span>.</span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><strong>Alex Frolov</strong><span>, CEO of HypeAuditor, is giving a talk about data-driven creator marketing. If you work in influencer marketing and know some gossip, you know </span><em>HypeAuditor is basically the Cloudflare</em><span> </span><em>of this industry</em><span>. Pretty much everything runs through it.</span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><strong>Sara Di Costanzo</strong><span>, Sr Strategic Partnerships Manager, Platform and Agency at Pinterest, will talk about the </span><strong>Pinterest Predicts Report 2026</strong><span>. I don’t need to convince you on this one. I know my analytics, and everyone loves that report. A talk about it is already a win.</span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">These three guests are absolute bangers, but the rest of the speakers list is also stacked: Matt Navarra, Olivia Mae Hanlon, Mark Erickson, Brock Johnson, Arielle Berlinsky, Tomas Loucky, Latasha James, and Xandrina Allday.</p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Now, they didn’t even need to pay me to tell you this because I always share </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/6c169780-7da7-442b-bebf-e8eca4d4da73?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Metricool’s social media studies</a><span> whenever they come out. You can catch a first look at the new one by joining this free event. </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/6c169780-7da7-442b-bebf-e8eca4d4da73?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Every session</a><span> will have a live Q&amp;A at the end so there will be multiple opportunities for attendees to ask questions.</span></p>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">Date: December 10, 2025 • Time: 9 AM EST • Format: Free, virtual</p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/6c169780-7da7-442b-bebf-e8eca4d4da73?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Register</a><span> for the </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/6c169780-7da7-442b-bebf-e8eca4d4da73?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Unfiltered event</a><span> at no cost and join us live.</span></p>
</blockquote>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><hr style="margin: 32px 0;padding: 0;height: 1px;background: #e6e6e6;border: none;"/></div>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>U.S. envoy warns Canada to ‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/582c61e0-a84d-4300-be2f-8af9611ae6bb?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">seriously consider</a><span>’ avoiding anti-tariff ads.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Toy makers rush into ‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/7b861928-0bb2-44a6-85ea-feb69f190c42?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">blind box</a><span>’ trend for holidays following Labubu craze.</span></p></li>

CMOs who made the move explain why.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>At Loyola Marymount’s KXLU, </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/7f0f212c-a2c3-494d-88a1-5411b127cbdd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">College Radio</a><span> keeps its cool.</span></p></li>

<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Forget sports betting. </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/0314d9b0-939b-4ef1-8918-36d493e88d56?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Kalshi</a><span> wants you to wager on sneakers and Labubu dolls..</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/fdc56832-830b-4c4a-b205-f7123f1bc56d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Where</a><span> Do the Children Play?</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>The </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/25d4008d-cf2b-4ea8-ac3d-d8419278840e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Silicon Valley Beauty Brand</a><span> biohackers are obsessed with.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>CMOs see </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a8a5522a-a5d4-4c58-aa17-19f836a68c8e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">support for brand building fade</a><span> while ROI scrutiny rises: </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b99b2d6f-3b94-4ae6-9afd-fff6c265e8d4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">NIQ</a><span>.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Only </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b99b2d6f-3b94-4ae6-9afd-fff6c265e8d4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">69% of CMOs</a><span> say their CEO and chief financial officer (CFO) support long-term brand investment, a sharp decline from 80% last year.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Why </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/65ae3049-ae99-4b00-ac97-3423a77c038a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">socks in Japan</a><span> are a serious business.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Marketers move to bring </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/5a58c08f-305d-4e74-864e-667d13b5a7da?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">transparency</a><span> to creator and influencer fees.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Is fashion ready for the </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/4c372018-0749-49b0-8575-01bea61f3d3a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">AI Bubble</a><span> to burst?</span></p></li>
</ul>

<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;">What’s happening at The Social Juice?</p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>It’s the Las Vegas Grand Prix. I tried to curate some of the best brand activations so far at the </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/9f38d185-6db1-4d4f-b707-dd301f49100b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Sphere Vegas</a><span>. My biased take is that Google and LEGO keep dominating with their incredible activations.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/aadc0354-ab9a-4f39-85f2-9ca8b1ab5974?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Wicked</a><span> has partnered with over 400 brands and counting. They even opened </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/6e1a3dda-fa4a-4e2b-b75b-96a40eb1e488?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">an experience center?</a><span> to showcase their collaborations, and it’s just crazy. </span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Over 300 people reacted to this post, and people are divided. Some say it’s the culture; some say capitalism. Our friend Ashley did great videos on both topics: </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/09ef9f4c-26ae-40f9-8050-4b230880804a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Wicked</a><span> and </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a2a3b685-5476-4615-87e2-d7cce69219cc?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">brand love vs. capitalism</a><span>.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/29dcd1fe-c976-4183-b674-3e36cebf29cc?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Zootopia 2</a><span> also had good but minimal movie marketing moments. If you haven’t seen them, you should.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/5d7317e8-6e54-4b6f-ad62-6692fc59df6e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Christmas campaigns</a><span> have divided people this year, but the social media engagement winner seems to be </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/769cceee-7429-4307-8087-11db9a26ddc6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Waitrose</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>We didn’t get time to make the Stranger Things collaboration post yet, but the </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/24de952e-ddbb-4c73-b411-a12eb330df79?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">collaborations</a><span> listed below have some highs and lows. The </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/91c509a1-aecd-47c2-a0ae-37fc7c5527a5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">KFC activation</a><span> is my personal favourite. </span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f15a09a4-961a-49b6-97b0-c6584a761e27?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Sony Playstation</a><span> is celebrating different milestones in different regions with campaigns that remind me of 2000s marketing. Meanwhile, Xbox’s marketing strategy of “</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/6e56e3ba-8d2d-46ad-8d7f-173ead8777b4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">This is an Xbox</a><span>” is a mixed bag even for their biggest fans.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Coincidence or planned? The Ralph Lauren pop-up at Chelsea couldn’t be </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/dc25a9ea-9e04-4b51-9e47-bc22d9c0bc73?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">more timely</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;">And someone is planning a comeback?</p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/cbab4701-b053-43c1-858a-a0967f864f81?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">BeReal</a><span> OOH campaigns </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/93a7002d-2c75-4b19-a1bd-d8bc825beff9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">kick off</a><span> in Paris, New York.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d929096f-a705-41c9-a00d-6a3aad369b5c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Don’t get them a Yeti</a><span> says Yeti ad campaign.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>No, you’re not seeing things: ‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/158088a1-60ab-4413-be01-042d249ba079?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">UFO landings</a><span>’ and sightings of ‘mysterious creatures’ are happening across the world in real life as part of Sony’s latest PS5 push.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>“</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/201852ee-bb64-41c0-9c82-50d0e4775c92?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">A Very ALDI Holiday Pop-Up</a><span>” is scheduled for Dec. 3 as the discount grocer prepares flagship Manhattan store.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Victoria Beckham </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/3be89330-6a09-4f1c-ab18-545bf3670540?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">explores a sale</a><span> for beauty business.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/70cdf3a4-840b-4b56-86a9-0014e836f3b2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Timothee Chalamet</a><span> wasn’t kidding: “</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/eb313fd8-f540-403a-a065-cd3a13b2bd58?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Marty Supreme</a><span>” marketing onslaught </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/237646ff-afbb-4132-b94f-b8cc99cc81f9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">includes</a><span> Real Orange Blimp, Virtual Ones on Google.</span></p></li>
</ul>

</li>

Coca-Cola rebrands soda 24 years after it was discontinued.</span></p></li>

<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;"><p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>😹 Report Break: Salesforce reports that the 2025 Cyber Week, Thursday, November 27 through Monday, December 1, is expected to drive a record-breaking </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f9fc3454-2e8e-43ba-b79b-eea32d3f0a19?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">$334 billion in global sales</a><span>, $73 billion of which is attributed to the influence of AI and </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/3efdfef6-33a4-4a77-81ac-59be93f6ecf5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">agents</a><span> (up 22% from 2024). Retailers with agents on their own properties already experienced seven times the U.S. sales growth (13%) compared with those without (2%) in the last seven weeks.</span></p></blockquote>
